<script>
(function () {
  // Usercentrics service name (exactly as in the ruleset) -> cookie names; * = prefix
  var COOKIES_BY_SERVICE = {
    'Google Analytics':               ['_ga', '_ga_*', '_gid', '_gat*'],
    'Google Ads Conversion Tracking': ['_gcl_*'],
    'Meta Pixel':                     ['_fbp', '_fbc'],
    'TikTok Advertising':             ['_ttp', '_tt_enable_cookie', 'ttcsid*'],
    'Microsoft Clarity':              ['_uetsid', '_uetvid', '_uetmsclkid'],
    'Quantcast':                      ['__qca'],
    'Job ADX':                        ['jdx-user', 'jdx-visit', 'jdx-*']
  };

  // careers.fedex.com -> ['', '.careers.fedex.com', '.fedex.com']
  function domains() {
    var parts = location.hostname.split('.'), list = [''];
    for (var i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i++) list.push('.' + parts.slice(i).join('.'));
    return list;
  }

  function matches(name, pattern) {
    return pattern.slice(-1) === '*' ? name.indexOf(pattern.slice(0, -1)) === 0 : name === pattern;
  }

  function deleteCookies(patterns) {
    document.cookie.split(';').map(function (c) { return c.split('=')[0].trim(); }).filter(Boolean)
      .forEach(function (name) {
        if (!patterns.some(function (p) { return matches(name, p); })) return;
        domains().forEach(function (d) {
          document.cookie = name + '=; Max-Age=0; Path=/' + (d ? '; Domain=' + d : '');
        });
      });
  }

  // Turns the Usercentrics event payload into { 'Service Name': true/false }
  function readConsent(detail) {
    var out = {}, services = detail && (detail.services || (detail.consent && detail.consent.services));
    if (services) {
      Object.keys(services).forEach(function (k) {
        var s = services[k];
        out[s.name || k] = !!(s.consent && typeof s.consent === 'object' ? s.consent.given : s.consent);
      });
    } else if (detail) {
      Object.keys(detail).forEach(function (k) { if (typeof detail[k] === 'boolean') out[k] = detail[k]; });
    }
    return out;
  }

  function cleanUp(detail) {
    var consent = readConsent(detail);
    Object.keys(COOKIES_BY_SERVICE).forEach(function (service) {
      if (consent[service] === false) deleteCookies(COOKIES_BY_SERVICE[service]);
    });
  }

  window.addEventListener('UC_CONSENT', function (e) { cleanUp(e.detail); }); // current Usercentrics
  window.addEventListener('ucEvent', function (e) { cleanUp(e.detail); });    // older versions / custom window event
})();
</script>
